### [OpenAI forms Advisory Group on Mathematics and AI](https://openai.com/index/advisory-group-on-mathematics-and-ai) ⭐️ 4.0/10

OpenAI has announced the formation of an independent Advisory Group on Mathematics and Artificial Intelligence. The group&\#x27;s role is to guide the review and communication of emerging results in the field. This initiative represents a structural step in OpenAI&\#x27;s approach to validating and presenting new AI developments.

rss · OpenAI Blog · Sep 21, 12:00

**「Background」** As AI models have grown more capable, the research community has emphasized the need for independent verification of bold claims, particularly those involving mathematics and reasoning. Advisory groups are commonly used by tech organizations to provide external oversight and credibility to their scientific output.

**「Impact」** The announcement provides no specific technical findings or changes to OpenAI&\#x27;s development processes at this time. Its immediate effect is limited to establishing a framework for external review of future results.

**Tags**: `#OpenAI`, `#AI Governance`, `#Advisory Board`

<a id="item-tech-news-5"></a>
### [Higgsfield AI ships new video features using GPT-6 Astra](https://openai.com/index/higgsfield-from-prompt-to-production-with-astra) ⭐️ 3.0/10

Higgsfield AI has introduced new video creation capabilities powered by OpenAI&\#x27;s GPT-6 Astra model to streamline video ad production. The integration is designed to lower barriers for small businesses by simplifying the workflow from prompt to finished video content. This update highlights the practical application of advanced language models in commercial creative tools, focusing on speed and accessibility rather than foundational research advancements.

rss · OpenAI Blog · Sep 21, 12:00

**「Background」** GPT-6 Astra is part of OpenAI&\#x27;s latest generation of language models, designed to handle complex reasoning and creative tasks with improved efficiency. In the northern hemisphere, the September equinox marks the start of astronomical autumn. The event occurs when the Sun crosses the celestial equator moving southward, resulting in nearly equal lengths of day and night. This astronomical transition signals the seasonal shift from summer to autumn in regions north of the equator.

rss · BBC\(British Broadcasting Corporation\) · Sep 22, 00:05

**「About the equinox」** An equinox happens twice a year when the plane of Earth&\#x27;s equator passes through the center of the Sun. The September equinox, also called the autumnal equinox in the northern hemisphere, typically falls on September 22 or 23 and marks one of two moments when sunlight is distributed equally between the Northern and Southern Hemispheres.

A study highlights that certain occupations, such as air traffic control, can offer six-figure salaries without requiring a four-year college degree. However, these roles remain uncommon because they mandate rigorous certification or specialized training programs. The findings underscore a growing distinction between traditional skilled trades and high-paying technical careers that exist outside the conventional degree pathway.

rss · 华盛顿邮报\(The Washington Post\) · Sep 21, 10:00

**「Background」** While the path to a four-year university degree is the most traditional route to high earnings in the US, some technical roles bypass this requirement entirely. These positions often fill critical workforce gaps in sectors like aviation and infrastructure, where specialized skills are valued over academic credentials.

**「Impact」** Individuals seeking high-income careers without student debt may find viable alternatives in highly regulated technical fields. This trend could encourage more vocational and technical training programs to align with market demands for specialized, high-paying roles.

rss · 福克斯新闻\(Fox News\) · Sep 22, 00:52

**「Background」** An MCL is one of the major ligaments stabilizing the knee joint, commonly injured in sports through direct contact or awkward landing. High-low hits, where a player contacts an opponent above and below the knee simultaneously, are a known mechanism for knee ligament damage.

**「Impact」** If the MCL injury is confirmed, Dart could miss significant playing time, affecting the Giants&\#x27; quarterback situation and offensive planning. The severity of the tear will determine whether he requires only rest and rehabilitation or more extensive intervention.

Iran&\#x27;s Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps \(IRGC\) has declared readiness to launch a counteroffensive should the United States attack, according to a spokesperson. This escalation comes after President Donald Trump announced major combat operations against Iran on February 28, involving massive joint U.S.-Israeli strikes. The statement signals heightened military tensions in the region as Iran prepares for potential retaliation.

rss · ABC新闻\(American Broadcasting Company\) · Sep 21, 19:18

Brig. Gen. General Hossein Mohebbi, an IRGC spokesperson, made the counteroffensive statement in an interview with the semi-official Tasnim News Agency. The Strait of Hormuz remains a geopolitical flashpoint, with regional peace talks currently stalled following the joint U.S.-Israeli strikes announced by President Donald Trump on February 28, 2026.

### [UK air traffic control glitch causes further disruption](https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cqp80p78mxx5o?at_medium=RSS&amp;at_campaign=rss) ⭐️ ?/10

A second technical glitch in UK air traffic control has caused further flight disruptions, drawing criticism from airlines and prompting Prime Minister Andy Burnham to express &quot;even greater concern&quot; about the system and its leadership. The incident represents a recurring failure in the organization responsible for managing UK airspace, raising serious questions about operational reliability and governance.

rss · BBC\(British Broadcasting Corporation\) · Sep 21, 20:21

**「Background」** UK air traffic control is provided by NATS \(National Air Traffic Services\), which manages the airspace and flight data for commercial aviation across the United Kingdom. The current disruption follows a second technical failure at the organisation&\#x27;s control systems in less than two weeks, prompting Prime Minister Andy Burnham to express heightened concern about both the system&\#x27;s resilience and its leadership.

**「Impact」** The recurring technical failures directly affect passengers and airlines operating in UK airspace, contributing to flight cancellations and delays. This situation increases political and regulatory pressure on the organization running air traffic control to address systemic issues.

A police officer and a man were wounded in an exchange of gunfire on Sunday evening near the Sons of Jacob Synagogue in Belleville, Ontario, as the Jewish community marked Yom Kippur. The incident occurred at approximately 7:10 pm, about 110 miles east of Toronto. The man is hospitalized in life-threatening condition, while the officer&\#x27;s injuries were described as wounded but not life-threatening. Ontario&\#x27;s special investigations unit is looking into the event.

rss · 卫报\(The Guardian\) · Sep 21, 14:56

**「Background」** Yom Kippur is the holiest day in the Jewish calendar, observed with fasting and prayer. The Special Investigations Unit \(SIU\) is Ontario&\#x27;s civilian oversight body that investigates incidents involving police where there has been serious injury or death.

**「Impact」** This incident has drawn attention to safety at Jewish community gatherings on a major holy day. It may prompt increased security measures at synagogues across Ontario during high-profile religious observances.

### [Typhoon Dujuan expected to strengthen before hitting Izu Islands](https://www.theguardian.com/environment/2026/sep/21/weather-tracker-japan-typhoon-dujuan) ⭐️ ?/10

Tropical Storm Dujuan is expected to restrengthen into a typhoon by Tuesday morning as it moves toward Japan&\#x27;s Izu Islands, bringing sustained winds of up to 80mph \(129km/h\) and gusts near 100mph. The system briefly reached typhoon status earlier in the week before weakening to a tropical storm on Sunday. Although mainland Japan has avoided a direct hit, evacuation warnings are in place for the Izu Islands due to threats of heavy rain, flooding, landslides, and high coastal waves.

rss · 卫报\(The Guardian\) · Sep 21, 09:45

**「Background」** Dujuan is part of the Pacific typhoon season, which typically peaks in late summer and early autumn. The article also notes that the Atlantic hurricane season is currently unusually quiet, highlighting the contrast between tropical cyclone activity in the two basins.

**「Impact」** Residents of the Izu Islands face evacuation orders and immediate risks from flooding, landslides, and dangerous coastal conditions. Mainland Japan is expected to be spared a direct hit but may still experience外围 effects from the storm&\#x27;s broad circulation.

### [Australia live: Super-for-mortgage proposal and supplement ban headline news](https://www.theguardian.com/australia-news/live/2026/sep/22/anthony-albanese-new-york-united-nations-intergenerational-report-coalition-one-nation-ntwnfb) ⭐️ ?/10

Australia&\#x27;s live news feed on 22 September 2026 highlighted two separate developments: a Liberal MP is expected to propose allowing Australians to use superannuation to secure mortgages, and an ingredient commonly found in cold and flu supplements has been banned due to allergy risks. The coverage also recorded Treasurer Jim Chalmers declining to support a national royal commission into violence against women and children, saying the government would instead continue increasing resources to address underlying drivers such as housing insecurity and online misogyny. The items reflect ongoing policy debates about housing affordability, consumer product safety, and government responses to family and domestic violence.

rss · 卫报\(The Guardian\) · Sep 22, 01:16

**「Background」** Superannuation is Australia’s compulsory retirement savings system, and proposals to access it for purposes other than retirement often raise questions about long-term financial security. Cold and flu supplement regulations in Australia are overseen by the therapeutic goods authority, which can restrict or ban ingredients when evidence shows significant health risks such as severe allergic reactions.

**「Impact」** If adopted, the superannuation mortgage proposal would directly affect Australian homebuyers and retirees by changing how retirement savings can be used for housing, while the ingredient ban will affect manufacturers and consumers of affected cold and flu products. The government’s stance on the royal commission signals continued reliance on targeted funding rather than a formal national inquiry into violence against women and children.

The criminal proceedings against Andreas Scheuer have exposed the drama behind the failed German passenger car toll \(Pkw-Maut\) system. The trial highlights how political missteps led to the collapse of the proposed toll scheme, which had been intended to generate revenue for infrastructure while addressing public concerns about road usage fees. The failure of the toll represents a significant episode in recent German transport policy, with legal consequences for those involved in its flawed implementation.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 21, 05:16

**「Background」** Andreas Scheuer, the former German Transport Minister, is standing trial for perjury in connection with a failed passenger car toll scheme \(PKW-Maut\) that was scrapped after public backlash over exemptions for existing vehicles. The criminal proceedings focus on whether Scheuer gave deliberately false statements before the Bundestag&\#x27;s investigative committee examining the policy&\#x27;s collapse.

### [USA seek two new military bases in Greenland](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/politik/usa-unter-trump/liveticker-usa-unter-trump-unterzeichnung-von-groenland-abkommen-offenbar-am-dienstag-faz-19444916.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung reports that the United States wants to establish two new military bases in Greenland under the Trump administration. This development is linked to an apparent agreement expected to be signed on Tuesday. The reported move signals a potential increase in American military presence in the Arctic region.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 22, 01:02

**「Background」** Greenland is an autonomous territory within the Kingdom of Denmark, strategically located in the Arctic. The United States already maintains the Thule Air Base in Greenland, which was established during the Cold War. Interest in expanding U.S. military presence in the Arctic has grown due to increasing geopolitical competition and climate change opening new shipping routes.

**「Impact」** If realized, the establishment of two additional military bases would significantly expand U.S. strategic capabilities in the Arctic and alter the security dynamics of the region. Denmark and Greenland would need to navigate complex diplomatic considerations regarding the expansion of foreign military infrastructure on their territory.

FIFA President Gianni Infantino has responded to a leadership dispute by writing to all 211 member associations regarding criticism of his management style. Despite engaging with critics, he explicitly ruled out any possibility of his own resignation. The move underscores the ongoing internal power struggle within FIFA&\#x27;s governance structure.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 21, 21:13

**「Background」** Gianni Infantino has served as FIFA President since 2016, leading the organization through a period of governance reform following earlier corruption scandals. The current leadership struggle stems from significant criticism of his management style and the failure of the FIFA Forward Enterprise \(FFE\) project, which has triggered calls for an independent review of FIFA&\#x27;s governance system.

**「Impact」** Infantino&\#x27;s refusal to step down ensures continuity in FIFA&\#x27;s leadership during a period of internal friction, though the dispute highlights persistent governance challenges within the organization. Member associations will likely watch for whether this outreach translates into measurable shifts in administrative behavior or policy.

<a id="item-tech-blog-2"></a>
### [On Mentorship of Ideas](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/ETnAXnEg3kqYwuwtj/on-mentorship-of-ideas) ⭐️ 4.0/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 22, 01:18

**「Background」** The AI Safety community actively recruits newcomers and invites them to share ideas in public channels like BlueDot Slack and Apart Research Discord. However, the structural reality is that these spaces receive frequent posts that readers typically ignore, leaving early-career thinkers without feedback after taking the difficult step of sharing their work.

**「Solution」** The author proposes a low-effort mentorship heuristic of spending approximately ten minutes per day providing direct feedback. This time is divided into two categories: Option A involves simply acknowledging receipt and offering a reaction to good or interesting ideas, which costs nothing extra beyond the cognitive effort already expended while reading. Option B applies to broken or flawed posts where a clear, quick diagnosis—such as fixing scientific writing mistakes or adjusting CV focus—can save contributors anywhere from two hours to five years of implementation time. The author argues that mentors often mistakenly feel responsible for the long implementation phase, when in reality they only need to provide the brief diagnostic input. Despite the availability of readers, this practice is rarely habitual, partly because many posts appear AI-generated or require essay-length responses that discourage quick engagement.

**「Takeaway」** Structural silence toward newcomers is actively harmful because it disproportionately drives away non-traditional paths and transforms a community into an &\#x27;ideas museum&\#x27; rather than a collaborative space. Providing brief, ten-minute feedback is a high-leverage intervention that validates contributors without requiring significant time investment from experienced members.

**Tags**: `#mentorship`, `#AI safety`, `#community dynamics`, `#writing feedback`

---
